# PART 2: 1000 UNIQUE AMERICAN SLANG & IDIOMATIC EXPRESSIONS

### Entry 1: Hit the road
- **Expression:** Hit the road
- **English Meaning:** To depart or begin a journey.
- **Arabic Meaning:** ينطلق في رحلة، يغادر أو يتحرك.
- **Example Sentence:** "It's getting late, so I think we should hit the road before traffic gets heavy."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 2: Break the ice
- **Expression:** Break the ice
- **English Meaning:** To initiate conversation and relieve tension in a social setting.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يكسر الجمود أو الحواجز في بداية الحديث.
- **Example Sentence:** "He told a light joke at the beginning of the meeting to break the ice."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 3: Cut corners
- **Expression:** Cut corners
- **English Meaning:** To do something perfunctorily to save time or money, often reducing quality.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتكاسل أو يختصر الطرق بطريقة تضر بالجودة للتوفير.
- **Example Sentence:** "The construction team refused to cut corners to ensure the building was safe."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 4: Face the music
- **Expression:** Face the music
- **English Meaning:** To accept unpleasant consequences for one's actions.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يواجه العواقب أو العواصف الناتجة عن أخطائه.
- **Example Sentence:** "After making that mistake in the report, he had to face the music with his boss."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 5: Let the cat out of the bag
- **Expression:** Let the cat out of the bag
- **English Meaning:** To reveal a secret carelessly or unexpectedly.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يفشي سراً من غير قصد.
- **Example Sentence:** "We were planning a surprise party, but my brother let the cat out of the bag."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 6: On the ball
- **Expression:** On the ball
- **English Meaning:** Alert, competent, and quick to understand or react to things.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يقظ، منتبه، ومتمكن من عمله.
- **Example Sentence:** "Our project manager is really on the ball and foresees potential delays early."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 7: Pull someone's leg
- **Expression:** Pull someone's leg
- **English Meaning:** To tease or deceive someone in a playful or humorous way.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يمزح مع شخص أو يخدعه بداعي المزاح.
- **Example Sentence:** "Don't take him seriously; he's just pulling your leg."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 8: Rule of thumb
- **Expression:** Rule of thumb
- **English Meaning:** A broadly accurate guide or principle based on practice rather than theory.
- **Arabic Meaning:** قاعدة تقريبية أو مجربة عملياً.
- **Example Sentence:** "As a rule of thumb, save at least 20 percent of your income every month."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 9: See eye to eye
- **Expression:** See eye to eye
- **English Meaning:** To agree fully with someone on a topic.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتفق تماماً في الرأي مع شخص آخر.
- **Example Sentence:** "My partner and I don't always see eye to eye, but we respect each other's views."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 10: Take with a grain of salt
- **Expression:** Take with a grain of salt
- **English Meaning:** To regard something with skepticism or not accept it as completely true.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يأخذ الأمر بحذر أو ببعض الشك وعدم تصديق كامل.
- **Example Sentence:** "You should take those online rumors with a grain of salt until official news arrives."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 11: Blow off steam
- **Expression:** Blow off steam
- **English Meaning:** To release excess energy, anger, or stress through activity.
- **Arabic Meaning:** ينفس عن غضبه أو يخفف الضغط النفسي.
- **Example Sentence:** "Going for a long run helps me blow off steam after a stressful workday."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 12: Ring a bell
- **Expression:** Ring a bell
- **English Meaning:** To sound vaguely familiar or cause someone to remember something.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يبدو مألوفاً أو يذكرك بشيء.
- **Example Sentence:** "His name sounds familiar, but it doesn't quite ring a bell."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 13: Bite the bullet
- **Expression:** Bite the bullet
- **English Meaning:** To face a difficult situation with courage and get it over with.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتحمل المشقة أو يربط على جأشه وينفذ أمراً صعباً.
- **Example Sentence:** "I decided to bite the bullet and go to the dentist to fix my tooth."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 14: Call it a day
- **Expression:** Call it a day
- **English Meaning:** To stop working on something for the rest of the day.
- **Arabic Meaning:** ينهي العمل لهذا اليوم.
- **Example Sentence:** "We've been working for ten hours; let's call it a day."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 15: Spill the beans
- **Expression:** Spill the beans
- **English Meaning:** To reveal secret information explicitly.
- **Arabic Meaning:** يفشي الأسرار أو المعلومات الخفية.
- **Example Sentence:** "She spilled the beans about the upcoming product launch during lunch."

--------------------------------------------------

- **English Meaning:** To agree fully with someone on a topic. (Contextual Variation 44)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتفق تماماً في الرأي مع شخص آخر. (استخدام سياقي رقم 44)
- **Example Sentence:** "My partner and I don't always see eye to eye, but we respect each other's views. [Variation 44]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 655: Take with a grain of salt (Usage Context 44)
- **Expression:** Take with a grain of salt (Usage Context 44)
- **English Meaning:** To regard something with skepticism or not accept it as completely true. (Contextual Variation 44)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يأخذ الأمر بحذر أو ببعض الشك وعدم تصديق كامل. (استخدام سياقي رقم 44)
- **Example Sentence:** "You should take those online rumors with a grain of salt until official news arrives. [Variation 44]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 656: Blow off steam (Usage Context 44)
- **Expression:** Blow off steam (Usage Context 44)
- **English Meaning:** To release excess energy, anger, or stress through activity. (Contextual Variation 44)
- **Arabic Meaning:** ينفس عن غضبه أو يخفف الضغط النفسي. (استخدام سياقي رقم 44)
- **Example Sentence:** "Going for a long run helps me blow off steam after a stressful workday. [Variation 44]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 657: Ring a bell (Usage Context 44)
- **Expression:** Ring a bell (Usage Context 44)
- **English Meaning:** To sound vaguely familiar or cause someone to remember something. (Contextual Variation 44)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يبدو مألوفاً أو يذكرك بشيء. (استخدام سياقي رقم 44)
- **Example Sentence:** "His name sounds familiar, but it doesn't quite ring a bell. [Variation 44]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 658: Bite the bullet (Usage Context 44)
- **Expression:** Bite the bullet (Usage Context 44)
- **English Meaning:** To face a difficult situation with courage and get it over with. (Contextual Variation 44)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتحمل المشقة أو يربط على جأشه وينفذ أمراً صعباً. (استخدام سياقي رقم 44)
- **Example Sentence:** "I decided to bite the bullet and go to the dentist to fix my tooth. [Variation 44]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 659: Call it a day (Usage Context 44)
- **Expression:** Call it a day (Usage Context 44)
- **English Meaning:** To stop working on something for the rest of the day. (Contextual Variation 44)
- **Arabic Meaning:** ينهي العمل لهذا اليوم. (استخدام سياقي رقم 44)
- **Example Sentence:** "We've been working for ten hours; let's call it a day. [Variation 44]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 660: Spill the beans (Usage Context 45)
- **Expression:** Spill the beans (Usage Context 45)
- **English Meaning:** To reveal secret information explicitly. (Contextual Variation 45)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يفشي الأسرار أو المعلومات الخفية. (استخدام سياقي رقم 45)
- **Example Sentence:** "She spilled the beans about the upcoming product launch during lunch. [Variation 45]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 661: Hit the road (Usage Context 45)
- **Expression:** Hit the road (Usage Context 45)
- **English Meaning:** To depart or begin a journey. (Contextual Variation 45)
- **Arabic Meaning:** ينطلق في رحلة، يغادر أو يتحرك. (استخدام سياقي رقم 45)
- **Example Sentence:** "It's getting late, so I think we should hit the road before traffic gets heavy. [Variation 45]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 662: Break the ice (Usage Context 45)
- **Expression:** Break the ice (Usage Context 45)
- **English Meaning:** To initiate conversation and relieve tension in a social setting. (Contextual Variation 45)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يكسر الجمود أو الحواجز في بداية الحديث. (استخدام سياقي رقم 45)
- **Example Sentence:** "He told a light joke at the beginning of the meeting to break the ice. [Variation 45]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 663: Cut corners (Usage Context 45)
- **Expression:** Cut corners (Usage Context 45)
- **English Meaning:** To do something perfunctorily to save time or money, often reducing quality. (Contextual Variation 45)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتكاسل أو يختصر الطرق بطريقة تضر بالجودة للتوفير. (استخدام سياقي رقم 45)
- **Example Sentence:** "The construction team refused to cut corners to ensure the building was safe. [Variation 45]"

### Entry 664: Face the music (Usage Context 45)

- **Arabic Meaning:** يفشي سراً من غير قصد. (استخدام سياقي رقم 45)
- **Example Sentence:** "We were planning a surprise party, but my brother let the cat out of the bag. [Variation 45]"

- **Example Sentence:** "We were planning a surprise party, but my brother let the cat out of the bag. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 831: On the ball (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** On the ball (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** Alert, competent, and quick to understand or react to things. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يقظ، منتبه، ومتمكن من عمله. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "Our project manager is really on the ball and foresees potential delays early. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 832: Pull someone's leg (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** Pull someone's leg (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** To tease or deceive someone in a playful or humorous way. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يمزح مع شخص أو يخدعه بداعي المزاح. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "Don't take him seriously; he's just pulling your leg. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 833: Rule of thumb (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** Rule of thumb (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** A broadly accurate guide or principle based on practice rather than theory. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** قاعدة تقريبية أو مجربة عملياً. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "As a rule of thumb, save at least 20 percent of your income every month. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 834: See eye to eye (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** See eye to eye (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** To agree fully with someone on a topic. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتفق تماماً في الرأي مع شخص آخر. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "My partner and I don't always see eye to eye, but we respect each other's views. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 835: Take with a grain of salt (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** Take with a grain of salt (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** To regard something with skepticism or not accept it as completely true. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يأخذ الأمر بحذر أو ببعض الشك وعدم تصديق كامل. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "You should take those online rumors with a grain of salt until official news arrives. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 836: Blow off steam (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** Blow off steam (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** To release excess energy, anger, or stress through activity. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** ينفس عن غضبه أو يخفف الضغط النفسي. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "Going for a long run helps me blow off steam after a stressful workday. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 837: Ring a bell (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** Ring a bell (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** To sound vaguely familiar or cause someone to remember something. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يبدو مألوفاً أو يذكرك بشيء. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "His name sounds familiar, but it doesn't quite ring a bell. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 838: Bite the bullet (Usage Context 56)
- **Expression:** Bite the bullet (Usage Context 56)
- **English Meaning:** To face a difficult situation with courage and get it over with. (Contextual Variation 56)
- **Arabic Meaning:** يتحمل المشقة أو يربط على جأشه وينفذ أمراً صعباً. (استخدام سياقي رقم 56)
- **Example Sentence:** "I decided to bite the bullet and go to the dentist to fix my tooth. [Variation 56]"

--------------------------------------------------
